+
+fn diagnostic(source: &str, code: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Diagnostic {
+    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    bound
+        .diagnostics
+        .iter()
+        .find(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == code)
+        .cloned()
+        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("missing {code}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics))
+}
+
+fn replace_and_compile(source: &str, authored: &str, replacement: &str) {
+    let repaired = source.replacen(authored, replacement, 1);
+    assert_ne!(repaired, source, "fixture does not contain {authored:?}");
+    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired)]);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn sql_habits_name_quarry_order_and_emit_compiling_rewrites() {
+    let count = "table logs:\n  kind: text <=20\n  public 'logs'\nquery total():\n  return count logs where .kind = 'error'\n";
+    let d = diagnostic(count, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
+    assert!(d.message.contains("aggregates take parentheses"), "{d}");
+    replace_and_compile(count, "count logs where .kind = 'error'", &d.rewrite);
+
+    let descending = "table logs:\n  at: time\n  public 'logs'\nquery recent():\n  return logs last 20 by .at desc\n";
+    let d = diagnostic(descending, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
+    assert!(d.message.contains("last 20 by .at") && d.message.contains("descending"), "{d}");
+    replace_and_compile(descending, "logs last 20 by .at desc", &d.rewrite);
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn cross_table_identity_names_the_wrong_reference_before_scan_advice() {
+    let source = "table secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(secret: secrets):\n  r = secrets[secret] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  return exists rolls[r.id]\n";
+    let d = diagnostic(source, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
+    assert!(d.message.contains("`r.id` is a secrets reference"), "{d}");
+    assert!(d.message.contains("rolls is keyed by its own id"), "{d}");
+
+    let ambiguous = "table secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  primary: secrets\n  backup: secrets\n  unique primary\n  unique backup\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(secret: secrets):\n  return rolls[secret]\n";
+    let d = diagnostic(ambiguous, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
+    assert!(d.message.contains("candidate keys"), "{d}");
+    assert!(!d.message.contains("keyed by its own id"), "{d}");
+
+    let later_mismatch = "table secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  secret: secrets\n  number: int\n  unique secret, number\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(secret: secrets):\n  return rolls[secret, 'bad']\n";
+    let d = diagnostic(later_mismatch, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
+    assert!(d.message.contains("no declared unique key accepting these positional argument types"), "{d}");
+    assert!(!d.message.contains("is a secrets reference") && !d.message.contains("keyed by its own id"), "{d}");
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn positional_type_selection_keeps_row_to_id_context_after_choosing_by_id() {
+    for key in ["row", "(row if choose else row)"] {
+        let source = format!(
+            "table items:\n  public 'items'\nquery present(id: items, choose: bool):\n  row = items[id] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  return exists items[{key}]\n"
+        );
+        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
+        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{key}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn invalid_search_keeps_the_table_symbol_for_later_references() {
+    let source = "table businesses:\n  category: int\n  search category\n  public 'businesses'\nquery one(id: businesses):\n  return businesses[id]\n";
+    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.message.contains("search category requires a text or enum column on businesses")), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
+    assert!(!bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.message.contains("unknown table businesses")), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn multiline_require_rewrite_preserves_the_authored_refusal_code() {
+    let source = "query choose(a: bool, b: bool):\n  require (a or\n    b)\n    else NOPE\n  return 1\n";
+    let d = diagnostic(source, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
+    assert!(d.rewrite.contains("else NOPE"), "{d}");
+    assert!(!d.rewrite.contains("NOT_ALLOWED"), "{d}");
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn reviewed_disclosure_silences_only_the_maintain_delta_note() {
+    let source = "table events:\n  owner: principal\n  category: text <=20\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  update <- allow\nmaintain totals: count events by category\n  read <- allow\n";
+    let warned = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    assert!(warned.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"), "{:#?}", warned.notes);
+    let reviewed = compile(&[(
+        "schema.q".into(),
+        source.replace("  read <- allow\n", "  read <- allow\n  disclosure reviewed 'product staff intentionally see category totals'\n"),
+    )]);
+    assert!(reviewed.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", reviewed.diagnostics);
+    assert!(reviewed.notes.iter().all(|note| note.code != "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"), "{:#?}", reviewed.notes);
+
+    for line in [
+        "  disclosure reviewed ''\n",
+        "  disclosure reviewed 'first review'\n  disclosure reviewed 'second review'\n",
+    ] {
+        let rejected = compile(&[(
+            "schema.q".into(),
+            source.replace("  read <- allow\n", &format!("  read <- allow\n{line}")),
+        )]);
+        assert!(rejected.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX"), "{:#?}", rejected.diagnostics);
+    }
+}

+#[test]
+fn structural_fanout_leads_with_a_direct_viewer_predicate() {
+    let source = "table bottles:\n  organizer: principal\n  read <- .organizer = viewer\n  update <- .organizer = viewer\ntable shares:\n  bottle: bottles\n  member: principal\n  organizer: principal\n  cap 120 by bottle\n  read <- .member = viewer or .organizer = viewer\n  update <- .organizer = viewer\nmutation cancel(bottle: bottles):\n  b = bottles[bottle] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require b.organizer = viewer else NOT_ORGANIZER\n  for share in shares where .bottle = bottle:\n    update shares[share.id] { member: share.member }\n";
+    let d = diagnostic(source, "E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT");
+    assert!(d.rewrite.starts_with("First add `.member = viewer`"), "{d}");
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn unreadable_probe_names_an_existing_require_read_path() {
+    let source = "table notes:\n  author: principal\n  read <- .author = viewer\ntable gatecodes:\n  note: notes\n  unique note\n  read <- exists notes[.note, author = viewer]\nmutation setCode(note: notes):\n  row = notes[note] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require row.author = viewer else NOT_AUTHOR\n  if exists gatecodes[note]:\n    return 1\n  return 0\n";
+    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    let note = bound.notes.iter().find(|note| note.code == "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE")
+        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{:#?}", bound.notes));
+    assert!(note.message.contains("require/read path already established a readable notes row"), "{note}");
+
+    for guard in [
+        "require not exists notes[note] else PRESENT",
+        "require exists notes[note] or other else NOT_ALLOWED",
+    ] {
+        let source = format!("table notes:\n  author: principal\n  read <- .author = viewer\ntable gatecodes:\n  note: notes\n  unique note\n  read <- exists notes[.note, author = viewer]\nmutation setCode(note: notes, other: bool):\n  {guard}\n  if exists gatecodes[note]:\n    return 1\n  return 0\n");
+        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
+        let note = bound.notes.iter().find(|note| note.code == "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE")
+            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{guard}: {:#?}", bound.notes));
+        assert!(!note.message.contains("require/read path already established"), "{guard}: {note}");
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn delete_restrict_note_opens_the_matching_guide_section() {
+    let source = "table parents:\n  read <- allow\n  delete <- allow\ntable children:\n  parent: parents\n  read <- allow\nmutation drop(parent: parents):\n  delete parents[parent]\n";
+    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    let note = bound.notes.iter().find(|note| note.code == "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED")
+        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{:#?}", bound.notes));
+    assert!(note.rewrite.contains("guide writes --section \"On delete restrict\""), "{note}");
+}

//! Shared type-directed positional point selection for programs and rules.
// @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — identity wins; otherwise ordered compatible declared keys only.
use super::catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo};
use crate::{ast::{IndexDecl, ReadExpr}, diag::Diagnostic, types::Ty};

pub(super) fn candidates<'a>(info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, types: &[(Ty, bool)], synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>) -> Vec<&'a IndexDecl> {
    let identity = read.index.is_none() && types.len() == 1 &&
        (types[0].0.required() == Ty::Id || types[0].0.required() == Ty::Ref(read.table.clone())
            || (!types[0].0.is_optional() && matches!(types[0].0.required(),Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==read.table)));
    info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| !synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
        .filter(|index| match &read.index {
            Some(name) => &index.name == name,
            None if identity => index.name == "byId",
            None => index.unique && index.columns.len() == types.len(),
        })
        // Explicit indexes keep their existing mismatch diagnostics and range semantics.
        .filter(|index| read.index.is_some() || types.iter().zip(&index.columns).all(|((ty, literal), column)| {
            compatible(ty, *literal, &column_ty(info, index, column))
        })).collect()
}

fn column_ty(info: &TableInfo, index: &IndexDecl, column: &str) -> Ty {
    if index.computed.contains_key(column) { Ty::Int }
    else if column == "id" { Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone()) }
    else { info.columns.get(column).map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown) }
}

fn compatible(ty: &Ty, literal: bool, expected: &Ty) -> bool {
    ty.comparable(expected)
        || (!ty.is_optional() && matches!((ty.required(), expected.required()), (Ty::Row(a) | Ty::WriteRow(a), Ty::Ref(b)) if a == b))
        || (literal && expected.is_identity())
}

pub(super) fn refusal(
    info: &TableInfo,
    read: &ReadExpr,
    types: &[(Ty, bool)],
    candidates: &[&IndexDecl],
    catalog: &Catalog,
) -> Diagnostic {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — lead a wrong-table positional lookup with the foreign reference's actual table.
    if candidates.is_empty() && let Some((position, source)) = types.iter().enumerate().find_map(|(position, (ty, literal))| {
        match ty.required() {
            Ty::Ref(source) | Ty::Row(source) | Ty::WriteRow(source) if source != read.table
                && !info.indexes.iter()
                    .filter(|index| !catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
                    .filter(|index| read.index.as_ref().is_some_and(|name| name == &index.name)
                        || (read.index.is_none() && index.unique && index.columns.len() == types.len()))
                    .any(|index| index.columns.get(position)
                        .is_some_and(|column| compatible(ty, *literal, &column_ty(info, index, column)))) => Some((position, source)),
            _ => None,
        }
    }) {
        let value = read.keys.get(position).map(|key| crate::print::expression(&key.value)).unwrap_or_else(|| "this value".into());
        return Diagnostic::new(
            &read.site,
            "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN",
            format!("`{value}` is a {source} reference; {} is keyed by its own id, so a reference from another table cannot select it", read.table),
            format!("pass a {} id or reference; if this is a population read, name its predicate with `{} where .id = <{} id> first 20 by .id`", read.table, read.table, read.table),
        )
        .guide("reads");
    }
    let alternatives = if candidates.is_empty() && read.index.is_some() {
        info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| !catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>()
    } else { candidates.to_vec() };
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.7 — `budgets['general']` (lane 020): a text value names the declared
    // text key far more often than a row id, so that named form leads (and is --fix's choice).
    let mut alternatives = alternatives;
    alternatives.sort_by_key(|index| index.columns.iter().any(|column| column == "id"));
    let mut changes = Vec::new();
    for index in &alternatives {
        // Same-column opposite-order indexes need the explicit name as well.
        let duplicate = info.indexes.iter().any(|other| other.name != index.name && other.unique
            && other.columns.len() == index.columns.len() && other.columns.iter().all(|c| index.columns.contains(c)));
        if read.index.is_none() { changes.push(crate::repair::ReadChange::NamedKey { columns: index.columns.clone(), index: duplicate.then(|| index.name.clone()) }); }
        // Unknown explicit indexes and computed keys retain verified legacy alternatives.
        changes.push(crate::repair::ReadChange::Index(index.name.clone()));
    }
    let message = if alternatives.is_empty() {
        format!("{} has no declared unique key accepting these positional argument types in order; name the key columns, or use where for a population", read.table)
    } else {
        format!("{} positional argument types leave {} candidate keys; name the intended key columns or choose a compatible unique index explicitly", read.table, candidates.len())
    };
    let diagnostic = Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", message, "").guide("reads").repair_read(&read.site, changes);
    // The user explicitly opts into one context-verified choice with --fix.
    if read.index.is_none() && candidates.len() > 1 { diagnostic.mechanical() } else { diagnostic }
}

            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value });
            out.push(Stmt::If {
                condition: Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) },
                then_body: vec![Stmt::Let { name: tmp.clone(), value: zero }],
                else_body: vec![Stmt::Let { name: tmp.clone(), value: ir::field(ir::var(&row), "value") }],
            });
            let ty = info.columns.get("value").map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Int);
            let ty = self.maintained_result_type(maintain, read, ty);

        }
        Ok((value, Ty::Row(read.table.clone()).optional()))
    }

    fn scan(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Scan, u32)> {
        self.scan_at(read, out, prefix::Position::Page)
    }

    fn scan_at(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body, position: prefix::Position) -> Fallible<(Scan, u32)> {
        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
        let (keys, index, selection) = self.select_index(info, read, ReadMode::Range, out)?;
        let prefix = self.order_keys(info, &index, keys)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5.1 — one pinned parent authorizes all of its aggregate groups.
        if let Some(m) = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table).filter(|m| m.groups_by_parent(self.schema)) {
            let parent = self.schema.tables[&m.source].through.as_ref().unwrap();
            if m.parent_key.is_none() || !index.columns.iter().take(prefix.len()).any(|f| f == &parent.reference) {
                let fields = m.by.iter().filter(|f| *f != &parent.reference).map(|f| format!(".{f}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
                let order = if fields.is_empty() { ".id" } else { &fields };
                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_READ_THROUGH_SCAN",
                    format!("{} must pin {} to one readable {} row before enumerating groups", read.table, parent.reference, parent.parent),
                    format!("inside a page of {}, read `{}[{} = .id] by {order}` in its shape: `{{ *, totals: {}[{} = .id] by {order} }}`; keep the parent page's audience key, order and cursor. For a scalar supply every group key: {}[{}]. Omit an explicit aggregate read rule to inherit parent authorization", parent.parent, read.table, parent.reference, read.table, parent.reference, read.table, m.by.join(", "))).guide("maintains"));
            }
        }
        let (range, bound) = self.prefix_bound(read, &index, position)?;
        if info.maintained.is_some() && let Some(position) = index.columns.iter().position(|field| field == "value") {
            if prefix.len() != position {
                return Err(super::maintains::refusal(&range.site, format!("{} by {} needs every ranking scope field pinned: {}", read.table, index.name, index.columns[..position].join(", ")),
                    format!("pin {} with where equalities, then read {} last {} by .value after c; omit the scope predicate for a global ranking", index.columns[..position].join(", "), read.table, range.take)));
            }
        }
        if range.take == 0 {
            return Err(type_error(&range.site, "a page holds at least one row", print::read_expr(&ReadExpr { range: Some(ast::RangeSpec { take: 50, ..range.clone() }), ..read.clone() })));
        }
        let mut page_source = read.clone();
        page_source.computed.extend(index.computed.iter().map(|(key, value)| {
            let mut value = value.clone();
            if let A::Call { args, .. } = &mut value
                && let Some(A::Name { name, site }) = args.first().cloned() {
                args[0] = A::Member { base: Box::new(A::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name, site };
            }
            (key.clone(), value)
        }));
        for (key, column) in page_source.keys.iter_mut().zip(&index.columns) {
            key.column.get_or_insert_with(|| column.clone());
        }
        let filter_text = read.filter.as_ref().map(|expr| self.catalog.source.expression(expr));
        let (read, filter) = self.scan_filter(read, &index)?;
        let read = &read;
        // Key expressions precede the seek bounds and cursor, including their setup.
        let prefix: Vec<Expr> = prefix.into_iter().map(|value| self.snapshot(value, out)).collect();
        let seek = self.seek(info, read, &index, out)?;
        let mut after = None;
        if let Some(cursor) = &range.cursor {
            let (e, t) = self.expr(cursor, out)?;
            if !matches!(t.required(), Ty::Cursor) {
                return Err(type_error(cursor.site(), format!("after expects a cursor, got {}", t.name()), "declare c: cursor ? and pass after c"));
            }
            if !info.total_cursor(&index) {
                let columns = index.columns.iter().map(|column| index.computed.get(column)
                    .map(print::expression).unwrap_or_else(|| column.clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
                if index.unique {
                    // Do not add id to the unique constraint: that would
                    // weaken it. Plan a separate pageable index from the read.
                    let mut planned = read.clone();
                    let rewrite = if index.computed.is_empty() {
                        planned.index = None;
                        for (key, column) in planned.keys.iter_mut().zip(&index.columns) { key.column.get_or_insert_with(|| column.clone()); }
                        planned.order_keys = index.columns.iter().skip(prefix.len()).cloned().collect();
                        print::read_expr(&planned)
                    } else {
                        let mut name = format!("{}Page", index.name);
                        while info.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.name == name) { name.push('_'); }
                        planned.index = Some(name.clone());
                        format!("add `by {name}: {columns}, id` to {}; read `{}`", read.table, print::read_expr(&planned))
                    };
                    return Err(Diagnostic::new(&range.site, "E_CURSOR_INDEX", format!("index {}.{} cannot carry a cursor: its nullable unique key allows tied null rows without an id tiebreak", read.table, index.name), rewrite).guide("indexes"));
                }
                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&range.site, "E_CURSOR_INDEX", format!("index {}.{} cannot carry a cursor: it has no declared id or total unique key", read.table, index.name), format!("by {}: {columns}, id", index.name)).guide("indexes").mechanical());
            }
            after = Some(Box::new(e));

            // The search text follows the keys as the word term; the index
            // was chosen for its words component in exactly that position.
            let (e, t) = self.expr(matching, out)?;

                let rewrite = if matches!(t, Ty::Optional(ref inner) if matches!(**inner, Ty::Text(_))) { format!("{} ?? ''", print::expression(matching)) } else { "matching q  -- with q: text <=100".to_owned() };

            }
            prefix.push(IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(e));
        }

        self.page_sources.insert(scan_site.clone(), page_source);
        let pins = read.keys.iter().enumerate().map(|(position, key)| {
            let column = key.column.as_ref().unwrap_or(&index.columns[position]);
            format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
        let domain = if pins.is_empty() { read.table.clone() } else { format!("{} where {pins}", read.table) };
        self.sources.insert(format!("domain/{scan_site}"), (read.site.clone(), domain));
        if filter.is_some() && let Some(text) = filter_text { self.filters.insert(scan_site.clone(), text); }
        // Only the generated scan itself, never an authored scan bound inside its keys, cursor or helpers.
        if self.expanding_feed.as_ref().is_some_and(|(feed, site)| *feed == read.table && *site == read.site) { self.timeline_scans.insert(scan_site.clone()); }
        self.record_selection(&scan_site, read, &index, if position == prefix::Position::Exists { "exists" } else { "page" }, &selection);
        // Authored spelling serves diagnostics; inferred-index residency sites
        // keep canonical bytes. Explicit-index search alternatives retain their
        // authored origin even when presence planning synthesizes a browse index.
        if !read.order_keys.is_empty() && let Some(text) = self.catalog.canonical_read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()) {
            self.canonical_sources.insert(scan_site.clone(), text.clone());
        }
        let spelling = if !read.order_keys.is_empty() { self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned() } else { None };
        self.sources.insert(scan_site.clone(), (read.site.clone(), spelling.unwrap_or_else(|| print::read_expr(&ReadExpr { index: None, range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), ..read.clone() }))));
        let mut scan = Scan { cursor_pins: read.cursor_prefix.map_or(0, |old| (read.keys.len() - old) as u32),
            variant: self.variant(),
            site: scan_site,
            table: read.table.clone(),
            index: index.name,
            prefix,
            order: if range.order == ast::Order::Asc { Order::Asc } else { Order::Desc },
            take: bound,
            existence: position == prefix::Position::Exists,
            after,
            before: None,
            range: seek,
            filter,
            opaque_cursor: range.cursor.is_some(),
            merge_site: None,
            provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape,
        };
        if position == prefix::Position::Aggregate && snapback4_core::engine::rules::scan_visibility(self.schema, &scan).uniform {
            scan.take = Bound::Meter;
        }
        if matches!(position, prefix::Position::Exists | prefix::Position::Aggregate) {
            self.constraint_domains.insert(scan.site.clone());
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.028#4-put-the-rules-where-the-builder-meets-them — job/preview advice belongs only to a helper with a query-ineligible meter read.
        if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.call_chain.len() > 1
            && scan.take == Bound::Meter && !snapback4_core::engine::rules::scan_visibility(self.schema, &scan).uniform {
            // Job domains always use the meter. A fitting pinned group cap instead
            // gives a query a literal bound, bypassing its non-uniform meter refusal.
            let cap = self.schema.group_bound(&scan.table, &index.columns[..read.keys.len()]);
            if cap.is_none_or(|cap| u64::from(cap) > snapback4_core::Ceilings::default().examined) {
                self.job_bound_helpers.insert(self.call_chain.last().unwrap().0.clone());
            }
        }
        if self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Job && (position != prefix::Position::Aggregate || self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Query) { self.check_meter_prefix(read, &scan, position == prefix::Position::Aggregate)?; }
        if position != prefix::Position::Aggregate { self.shape_meter_note(&scan, read); }
        Ok((scan, range.take))
    }

    /// Evaluate the keys, then choose the index: explicit `by`, else by
    /// key names, count and types. Point reads need a unique full key;
    /// ranges need a strict prefix.
    fn select_index(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>, ast::IndexDecl, String)> {
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — retain bound keys for type selection and final lowering.
        // No cloned expression pass: nested lookups must not multiply binder work.
        let mut keys = Vec::<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>::new();
        let mixed = read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_some()) && read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_none());
        let types = if mixed {
            let prefix = read.keys.iter().take_while(|key| key.column.is_none()).count();
            self.bind_key_parts(info, read, prefix, &mut keys, out)?;
            Some(keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>())
        } else { None };
        let mut normalized = super::mixed_keys::read_typed(info, read, self.catalog, types.as_deref())?;
        self.name_bound_keys(info, &normalized, &mut keys)?;
        let read = normalized.as_ref();
        super::validate_key_names(read)?;
        let mut positional_reason = None;
        if !read.keys.is_empty() && read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_none()) {
            let declared = |index: &&ast::IndexDecl| !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone()));
            let mut candidates = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).filter(|index| match &read.index {
                Some(name) => &index.name == name,
                None => index.unique && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len(),
            }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            if keys.is_empty() {
                self.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut keys, out)?;
            }
            let types = keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            if read.index.is_none() {
                candidates = super::positional_keys::candidates(info, read, &types, &self.catalog.synthesized);
            }
            if read.index.is_none() && mode == ReadMode::Range && read.order_keys.is_empty() && candidates.len() == 1 {
                let scan = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).find(|index| !index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.len() > read.keys.len());
                let columns = scan.map(|index| index.columns.as_slice()).unwrap_or(&candidates[0].columns);
                let predicate = read.keys.iter().zip(columns).map(|(key, column)| format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", "a positional key selects one declared unique row, not a scan prefix",
                    format!("{} where {predicate} first 20 by .id", read.table)).guide("reads"));
            }
            if candidates.len() != 1 {
                return Err(super::positional_keys::refusal(info, read, &types, &candidates, self.catalog));
            }
            if read.index.is_none() {
                positional_reason = Some(if info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len()).count() == 1 {
                    "only declared unique key of this arity"
                } else { "unique positional key selected by argument types" });
                normalized.to_mut().index = Some(candidates[0].name.clone());
            }
        }
        let read = normalized.as_ref();
        let point = mode != ReadMode::Range;
        if keys.is_empty() {
            self.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut keys, out)?;
        }
        // Type-directed positional selection may have bound these operands
        // before the index was known. Apply its row-to-id context without
        // rebinding or repeating any operand effects.
        self.contextualize_bound_keys(info, read, &mut keys);
        let named = keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| c.is_some()) && !keys.is_empty();
        let mut selection = if read.index.is_some() { "explicit by" } else if named { "named equality columns" } else { "compatible positional key types" };
        // A `words(..)` index serves one read: `matching` with exactly its
        // leading columns as keys. Every other read sees plain indexes only.
        let searching = mode == ReadMode::Range && read.matching.is_some();
        let mut candidates: Vec<&ast::IndexDecl> = info
            .indexes
            .iter()
            .filter(|index| read.index.as_ref().is_none_or(|explicit| explicit == &index.name))
            .filter(|index| read.seeks.iter().all(|b| index.columns.get(keys.len()) == Some(&b.column)))
            .filter(|index| if searching { index.searches_after(keys.len()) } else { index.words.is_none() })
            .filter(|index| match mode {
                ReadMode::Point | ReadMode::FilteredPoint => index.unique && (index.columns.len() == keys.len() || (named && mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() < keys.len())),
                ReadMode::Exists => (index.columns.len() == keys.len() && (named || read.index.is_some() || index.unique)) || (named && info.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() < keys.len()),
                ReadMode::Range => index.columns.len() > keys.len() || (!read.order_keys.is_empty() && index.unique && index.columns.len() == keys.len()),
            })
            .filter(|index| {
                if named {
                    if index.columns.len() < keys.len() { index.columns.iter().all(|column| keys.iter().any(|(c, _, _)| c.as_ref() == Some(column))) } else { keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| index.columns[..keys.len()].contains(c.as_ref().unwrap())) }
                } else {
                    true
                }
            })
            .collect();
        if named && matches!(mode, ReadMode::FilteredPoint | ReadMode::Exists) {
            if let Some(width) = candidates.iter().filter(|i| info.total_unique(i)).map(|i| i.columns.len()).min() {
                candidates.retain(|i| info.total_unique(i) && i.columns.len() == width);
                // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.7 — named equalities covering two total unique keys
                // (`loans[borrower = viewer, telescope = t]`, lane 057) test one row either
                // way, the other key's columns being extra equalities on it; the first
                // declared key decides, as a rule probe already does (§11).
                // Same-column keys in two orders (`unique pair: low, high` and `unique
                // inverse: high, low`) still need an explicit `by` (guide indexes).
                let mut sets = candidates.iter().map(|index| { let mut columns = index.columns.clone(); columns.sort(); columns }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                sets.dedup();
                if candidates.len() > 1 && sets.len() == candidates.len() {
                    selection = "first declared unique key the named equalities cover";
                    candidates.truncate(1);
                }
            } else if candidates.iter().any(|i| i.unique) {
                // Exact nullable unique keys still select points. A competing
                // nonunique index must not turn an exists probe into a tie.
                candidates.retain(|i| i.unique);
            }
        }
        // Positional keys select by type; a text literal may meet an
        // identity column (it becomes an id once the index is chosen).
        if !named {
            candidates.retain(|index| {
                keys.iter().enumerate().all(|(position, (_, e, t))| {
                    index.columns.get(position).is_some_and(|column| {
                        let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, column);
                        t.comparable(&expected) || (expected.is_identity() && matches!(e, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))
                    })
                })
            });
            // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.5 — resolve remaining ambiguity without replacing an existing exact-type winner.
            let exact: Vec<_> = candidates.iter().copied().filter(|index| keys.iter().enumerate().all(|(position, (_, _, t))| index.columns.get(position).is_some_and(|column| self.key_ty(info, index, column) == t.required()))).collect();
            if exact.len() == 1 {
                candidates = exact;
                if read.index.is_none() { selection = "single exact positional type match"; }
            }
            else {
                // Filter before the one-candidate arm so identity literals receive their index's type.
                let unique: Vec<_> = candidates.iter().copied().filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns.len() == keys.len()).collect();
                if unique.len() == 1 {
                    candidates = unique;
                    if read.index.is_none() { selection = "only compatible full unique key"; }
                }
            }
        }
        if let Some(explicit) = &read.index
            && candidates.is_empty()
        {
            let known = info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == explicit);
            return Err(match known {
                None => unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no index {explicit}", read.table), name_rewrite(explicit, info.indexes.iter().map(|i| i.name.as_str()), None)),
                Some(index) if index.words.is_some() && !searching => unknown(&read.site, format!("{}.{explicit} is a words index; it is read with matching", read.table), format!("{}[{}] matching q first 50 by {explicit}", read.table, index.columns[..index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p)].join(", "))),
                Some(index) if searching && index.words.is_none() => self.matching_nonwords_index(info, read, index),
                Some(index) if searching => Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_MATCHING_INDEX", format!("{}.{explicit} has its words after {} column{}; matching takes exactly those as keys", read.table, index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p), if index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p) == 1 { "" } else { "s" }), format!("{}[{}] matching q first 50 by {explicit}", read.table, index.columns[..index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p)].join(", "))),
                Some(index) => self.index_mismatch(info, read, mode, &keys, index),
            });
        }
        match candidates.len() {
            1 => {
                let index = candidates[0].clone();
                if mode == ReadMode::Range && read.index.is_none() && !read.keys.is_empty() && !named {
                    let predicate = read.keys.iter().zip(&index.columns).map(|(key, column)| format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
                    let order = index.columns.iter().skip(read.keys.len()).map(|column| format!(".{column}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
                    return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", format!("{} would select non-unique prefix {}", print::read_expr(read), index.name), format!("{} where {predicate} first 50 by {order}", read.table)).guide("reads"));
                }
                if !named {
                    for (position, (_, e, t)) in keys.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                        if let Some(column) = index.columns.get(position)
                            && self.key_ty(info, &index, column).is_identity()
                            && let Expr::Literal(Value::String(text)) = e
                        {
                            *e = Expr::Literal(Value::Id(text.clone()));
                            *t = Ty::Id;
//! Bind key operands once, retaining authored sequencing and contextual literals.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    pub(super) fn bind_key_parts(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, end: usize,
        keys: &mut Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<()> {
        for (position, part) in read.keys.iter().enumerate().take(end).skip(keys.len()) {
            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
            let expected = part.column.as_deref().or_else(|| read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == name)).and_then(|i| i.columns.get(position)).map(String::as_str)).and_then(|column| key_identity(info, column));
            let (e, t) = match expected {
                Some(expected) => self.expr_as_reference(&part.value, &mut setup, &expected)?,
                None => self.expr(&part.value, &mut setup)?,
            };
            self.sequence_setup(keys.iter_mut().map(|(_, e, _)| e), setup, out);
            let e = if expr::allocates(&e) { self.snapshot(e, out) } else { e };
            keys.push((part.column.clone(), e, t));
        }
        self.contextualize_bound_keys(info, read, keys);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Apply the selected index's identity context to operands already bound
    /// for type-directed positional selection. This changes only their
    /// retained IR/type and never evaluates the authored expression again.
    pub(super) fn contextualize_bound_keys(&self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr,
        keys: &mut [(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)]) {
        if let Some(index) = read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == name)) {
            for (position,(column,value,ty)) in keys.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                if let Some(expected) = column.as_deref().or_else(|| index.columns.get(position).map(String::as_str)).and_then(|c| key_identity(info,c)) {
                    (*value,*ty) = self.row_identity(value.clone(),ty.clone(),&expected);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Mixed-key normalization names an already-bound positional prefix. Only
    // a literal acquires new context; compound expressions keep their IR/setup.
    pub(super) fn name_bound_keys(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr,
        keys: &mut [(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)]) -> Fallible<()> {
        for ((column, expr, ty), part) in keys.iter_mut().zip(&read.keys) {
            *column = part.column.clone();
            if let Some(expected) = column.as_deref().and_then(|column| key_identity(info, column))
                && matches!(&part.value, A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(_), .. }) {
                (*expr, *ty) = self.expr_as_reference(&part.value, &mut setup::Body::default(), &expected)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn key_identity(info: &TableInfo, column: &str) -> Option<Ty> {
    let ty = if column == "id" { Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone()) }
        else { Ty::from_scalar(info.columns.get(column)?) };
    ty.required().is_identity().then_some(ty)
}

use snapback4_lang::compile;

#[test]
fn project_builtins_are_reserved_for_parameters_locals_and_loop_bindings() {
    for (source, name) in [
        ("query q(now: time):\n  return now\n", "now"),
        ("mutation m():\n  commit = now\n  return commit\n", "commit"),
        ("mutation m():\n  for new in range(0, 1):\n    return new\n", "new"),
        ("query q(viewer: principal):\n  return viewer\n", "viewer"),
    ] {
        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        let diagnostic = bound
            .diagnostics
            .iter()
            .find(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_RESERVED")
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{name}: {:?}", bound.diagnostics));
        assert!(diagnostic.message.contains(&format!("`{name}`")), "{diagnostic}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn repeated_identity_declarations_name_both_method_sets() {
    let same_file = compile(&[(
        "schema.q".into(),
        "use identity password\nuse identity guests\n".into(),
    )]);
    assert_eq!(same_file.diagnostics.len(), 1, "{:?}", same_file.diagnostics);
    let diagnostic = &same_file.diagnostics[0];
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.code, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.site.line, 2);
    assert!(
        diagnostic.message.contains("password") && diagnostic.message.contains("guests"),
        "{diagnostic}"
    );

    let across_files = compile(&[
        ("schema.q".into(), "use identity guests\n".into()),
        ("accounts.q".into(), "use identity\n".into()),
    ]);
    assert_eq!(across_files.diagnostics.len(), 1, "{:?}", across_files.diagnostics);
    let diagnostic = &across_files.diagnostics[0];
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.site.file, "accounts.q");
    assert!(
        diagnostic.message.contains("guests")
            && diagnostic.message.contains("password and guests"),
        "{diagnostic}"
    );
}

    fn analysis_expr(value: &Expr) -> Expr {
        let mut copy = value.clone(); super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy, &mut expand_shorthand); copy
    }
    fn analysis_body(body: &[Stmt]) -> Vec<Stmt> {
        let mut copy = body.to_vec(); super::intent_walk::statements(&mut copy, &mut expand_shorthand); copy
    }
    fn fact_text(value: &Expr) -> String {
        let mut value = analysis_expr(value);
        super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value, &mut |expr| {
            if is_viewer(expr) { *expr = Expr::Name { name: "viewer".into(), site: expr.site().clone() }; }
        });
        crate::print::expression(&value)
    }
    fn facts(condition: &Expr, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {
        if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = condition && op == "and" { facts(left, out); facts(right, out); }
        else { out.insert(fact_text(condition)); }
    }
    fn read_dependencies(rule: &RuleExpr, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {
        match rule {
            RuleExpr::Exists { table, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, .. } => {
                out.insert(table.clone());
            }
            RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => {
                for item in items { read_dependencies(item, out); }
            }
            RuleExpr::Not(rule) => read_dependencies(rule, out),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    fn readable(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema, read: &ReadExpr, known: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
        let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return true };
        if super::owner_equality::readable(catalog, schema, read, known) { return true; }
        let Some(info) = catalog.tables.get(&read.table) else { return table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable(); };
        let mut pinned = BTreeMap::new();
        // Unlike older advisory lints, proof cannot guess a non-id key: typed ids
        // may select byId even beside one same-width principal/composite key.
        let selected = read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|index| &index.name == name));
        let inferred: Vec<_> = info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len()).collect();
        let positional_index = if read.index.is_some() { selected.filter(|index| index.is_plain()) }
            else { match inferred.as_slice() { [index] => Some(*index), _ => None } };
        if let Some(index) = positional_index {
            for (column, key) in index.columns.iter().zip(&read.keys) {
                pinned.insert(key.column.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| column.clone()), key.value.clone());
            }
        }
        // Extra named tests pin fields even when the positional prefix is unresolved.
        if selected.is_none_or(|index| index.computed.is_empty()) {
            for part in &read.keys { if let Some(column) = &part.column { pinned.insert(column.clone(), part.value.clone()); } }
        }
        if read.index.is_none() && read.keys.iter().filter(|key| key.column.is_none()).count() == 1 && read.keys.first().is_some_and(|part| part.column.is_none() && is_viewer(&part.value)) {
            let principal_keys: Vec<_> = table.indexes.values().filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns().len() == 1
                && table.columns.get(&index.columns()[0]) == Some(&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).collect();
            if let [index] = principal_keys.as_slice() { pinned.insert(index.columns()[0].clone(), read.keys[0].value.clone()); }
        }
        let mut filters = Vec::new();
        if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { super::intent::split((**filter).clone(), &mut filters); }
        for filter in filters {
            if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = &filter && op == "="
                && let Some(column) = super::intent::column(left) { pinned.entry(column.to_owned()).or_insert_with(|| (**right).clone()); }
        }
        // A total self-membership key is witnessed by the probed row itself.
        if table.rules.read.disjuncts().iter().any(|rule| {
            let RuleExpr::Exists { table: target, index, key, equalities, .. } = rule else { return false };
            target == &read.table && equalities.is_empty() && table.index(index).is_some_and(|index|
                index.unique && index.columns().len() == key.len() && index.columns().iter().zip(key).all(|(column, value)| {
                    let required = column == "id" || table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| !matches!(kind, snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Optional(_)));
                    required && (matches!(value, RuleTerm::Field(field) if field == column)
                        || matches!(value, RuleTerm::Viewer) && pinned.get(column).is_some_and(is_viewer))
                }))
        }) { return true; }
        let term = |term: &RuleTerm| -> Option<String> {
            match term {
                RuleTerm::Viewer => Some("viewer".into()),
                RuleTerm::Literal(value) => Some(RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Literal(value.clone())).quarry().trim_start_matches("viewer = ").to_owned()),
                RuleTerm::Field(column) => pinned.get(column).map(fact_text),
                _ => None,
            }
        };
        fn holds(rule: &RuleExpr, term: &impl Fn(&RuleTerm) -> Option<String>, schema: &Schema, known: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
            match rule {
                RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Public { .. } => true,
                RuleExpr::Eq(a, b) => match (term(a), term(b)) {
                    (Some(a), Some(b)) => a == b || known.contains(&format!("({a} = {b})")) || known.contains(&format!("({b} = {a})")),
                    _ => false,
                },
                RuleExpr::And(items) => items.iter().all(|rule| holds(rule, term, schema, known)),
                RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().any(|rule| holds(rule, term, schema, known)),
                RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } => {
                    let Some(target) = schema.table(table).and_then(|t| t.index(index)) else { return false };
                    let Some(key) = key.iter().map(term).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>() else { return false };
                    let plain = format!("exists {table}[{}]", key.join(", "));
                    let Some(extra) = equalities.iter().map(|(c, t)| term(t).map(|v| format!("{c} = {v}"))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>() else { return false };
                    let named = target.columns().iter().zip(&key).map(|(c, v)| format!("{c} = {v}"))
                        .chain(extra).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
                    // Positional spellings with multiple compatible index widths
                    // are not evidence for this particular raw-rule key.
                    let source = schema.table(table).unwrap();
                    let unambiguous = source.indexes.values().filter(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.columns().len() == key.len()).count() == 1
                        || key.len() == 1 && key[0] == "viewer" && target.columns().len() == 1
                            && source.columns.get(&target.columns()[0]) == Some(&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)
                            && source.indexes.values().filter(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.columns().len() == 1
                                && source.columns.get(&candidate.columns()[0]) == Some(&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).count() == 1;
                    (equalities.is_empty() && unambiguous && known.contains(&plain)) || known.contains(&format!("exists {table}[{named}]"))
                },
                _ => false,
            }
        }
        holds(&table.rules.read, &term, schema, known)
    }
    fn guard(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema, program: &str, condition: &Expr, known: &BTreeSet<String>, system: bool, notes: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
        if system { return; }
        let mut reads = Vec::new();
        let mut copy = analysis_expr(condition);
        super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy, &mut |expr| {
            if let Expr::Exists { read, .. } = expr { reads.push(read.clone()); }
            if let Expr::Binary { op,left,right,.. } = expr && op == "=" {
                if let (Expr::Read(read),Expr::Literal { value:Literal::Null,.. }) | (Expr::Literal { value:Literal::Null,.. },Expr::Read(read)) = (left.as_ref(),right.as_ref()) { reads.push(read.clone()); }
            }
        });
        for read in reads {
            let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { continue };
            if readable(catalog, schema, &read, known) { continue; }
            if notes.iter().any(|note| note.site == read.site && note.code == "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" || note.site.file == read.site.file && note.site.line == read.site.line && note.code == "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" && note.message.starts_with(&format!("{program}: this probe reads {} under", read.table))) { continue; }
            let rule_site = rule_site(catalog, &read.table, Effect::Read).map(|site| format!(" ({site})")).unwrap_or_default();
            // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — acknowledge an existing readable path while retaining conservative probe advice.
            let mut rule_dependencies = BTreeSet::new();
            read_dependencies(&table.rules.read, &mut rule_dependencies);
            let established: Vec<_> = rule_dependencies.into_iter().filter(|dependency| {
                known.iter().any(|fact| fact.starts_with(&format!("exists {dependency}[")))
            }).collect();
            let path = if established.is_empty() {
                String::new()
            } else {
                format!(" This require/read path already established a readable {} row; the note remains because the compiler cannot prove that fact admits every {} row this negative probe must observe.", established.join(" or "), read.table)
            };
            notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE",
                format!("{program}: this probe reads {} under its read rule `{}`{rule_site}; rows hidden from the caller do not satisfy a program exists, so using this value as a negative guard cannot enforce absence across those rows (table-rule probes see all rows).{path}", read.table, table.rules.read.quarry()),
                "use a unique/cap/after insert constraint for an invariant across hidden rows, or authorize this caller to read the rows the guard must observe; keep this probe only when caller-visible existence is intended").guide("rules"));
        }
    }
    fn absence(condition: &Expr, inverted: bool) -> Vec<ReadExpr> {
        match condition {
            Expr::Exists { read, .. } => {
                let mut out = if inverted { vec![read.clone()] } else { vec![] };
                out.extend(absence(&Expr::Read(read.clone()), false)); out
            },
            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => absence(value, !inverted),
            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" || op == "!=" => {
                let pair = match (left.as_ref(), right.as_ref()) {
                    (value, Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(b), .. }) | (Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(b), .. }, value) => Some((value, *b)),
                    _ => None,
                };
                pair.map(|(value, b)| absence(value, inverted ^ (op == "=" && !b || op == "!=" && b))).unwrap_or_else(|| absence(left, inverted).into_iter().chain(absence(right, inverted)).collect())
            }
            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => absence(left, inverted).into_iter().chain(absence(right, inverted)).collect(),
            Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => {
                let mut all = Vec::new(); let mut copy = (**condition).clone();
                super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy, &mut |expr| { if let Expr::Exists { read, .. } = expr { all.push(read.clone()); } });
                all.extend(absence(then, inverted)); all.extend(absence(otherwise, inverted)); all
            }
            Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Member { base: value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. }
                | Expr::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } | Expr::Walk { seed: value, .. } => absence(value, inverted),
            Expr::Array { items, .. } | Expr::Call { args: items, .. } => items.iter().flat_map(|value| absence(value, inverted)).collect(),
            Expr::Object { fields, .. } => fields.iter().filter_map(|field| field.value.as_ref()).flat_map(|value| absence(value, inverted)).collect(),
            Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => absence(base, inverted).into_iter().chain(fields.iter().filter_map(|field| match field {
                ShapeField::Field { value, .. } => Some(value), _ => None,
            }).flat_map(|value| absence(value, inverted))).collect(),
            Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Created { read, .. } => {
                let mut values: Vec<_> = read.keys.iter().map(|key| &key.value).chain(read.seeks.iter().map(|key| &key.value)).collect();
                values.extend(read.filter.as_deref()); values.extend(read.matching.as_deref());
                values.extend(read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_deref()));
                values.into_iter().flat_map(|value| absence(value, false)).collect()
            },
            Expr::Write { keys, fields, condition, .. } => keys.iter().map(|key| &key.value)
                .chain(fields.iter().filter_map(|field| field.value.as_ref())).chain(condition.as_deref())
                .flat_map(|value| absence(value, inverted)).collect(),
            Expr::Merge(merge) => {
                let mut values: Vec<Expr> = [&merge.matching, &merge.filter, &merge.cursor].into_iter().filter_map(|value| value.as_deref().cloned()).collect();
                for lane in &merge.lanes {
                    values.extend(lane.admission.as_deref().cloned()); values.extend(lane.distinct.clone());
                    values.extend(lane.prefix.iter().cloned()); values.extend(lane.loop_.as_ref().map(|(_, value)| value.clone()));
                    for read in lane.planned.iter().chain(lane.nulls.as_ref().map(|(_, read)| read)) { values.push(Expr::Read(*read.clone())); }
                    if let Some(fields) = &lane.project { values.extend(fields.iter().filter_map(|field| if let ShapeField::Field { value, .. } = field { Some(value.clone()) } else { None })); }
                }
                values.iter().flat_map(|value| absence(value, inverted)).collect()
            },
            Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => vec![],
        }
    }
    // Reverse dependency closure identifies query values consumed by guards,
    // across aliases/objects and all possible rebinding paths. The observation
    // is checked at its original statement, with that statement's authority.
    fn has_guard(catalog: &Catalog, statements: &[Stmt], visiting: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
        if statements.iter().any(|stmt| matches!(stmt, Stmt::Require { .. } | Stmt::If { .. })) { return true; }
        let mut copy = analysis_body(statements); let mut found = false;
        super::intent_walk::statements(&mut copy, &mut |expr| {
            if matches!(expr, Expr::If { .. }) { found = true; }
            for (name, shape) in definitions(catalog, expr) {
                let key = format!("{shape}:{name}");
                if !visiting.insert(key.clone()) { found = true; continue; }
                found |= helper_body(catalog, &name, shape).is_some_and(|body| has_guard(catalog, &body, visiting));
                visiting.remove(&key);
            }
        });
        // Explicit guards can also be nested in loops/viewer scopes.
        found || statements.iter().any(|stmt| match stmt { Stmt::For { body, .. } | Stmt::As { body, .. } => has_guard(catalog, body, visiting), _ => false })
    }
    fn guarded_inputs(catalog: &Catalog, expr: &Expr) -> Vec<Expr> {
        let consumed = definitions(catalog, expr).iter().any(|(name, shape)| helper_body(catalog, name, *shape)
            .is_some_and(|body| has_guard(catalog, &body, &mut BTreeSet::from([format!("{shape}:{name}")]))));
        match expr {
            Expr::Call { args, .. } if consumed => args.clone(),
            Expr::ShapeUse { base, .. } if consumed => vec![(**base).clone()],
            Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => {
                let values: Vec<_> = fields.iter().filter_map(|field| if let ShapeField::Field { value, site, .. } = field { Some(Stmt::Expr { value: value.clone(), site: site.clone() }) } else { None }).collect();
                if consumed || has_guard(catalog, &values, &mut BTreeSet::new()) { vec![(**base).clone()] } else { vec![] }
            },
            _ => vec![],
        }
    }
    fn guard_bindings(catalog: &Catalog, statements: &[Stmt]) -> BTreeSet<String> {
        fn add_names(expr: &Expr, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {
            if let Expr::Name { name, .. } = expr { out.insert(name.clone()); }
            if let Expr::Object { fields, .. } | Expr::Write { fields, .. } = expr {
                out.extend(fields.iter().filter(|field| field.value.is_none() && !field.spread).map(|field| field.name.clone()));
            }
        }
        fn names(value: &Expr) -> BTreeSet<String> {
            let mut out = BTreeSet::new(); let mut copy = analysis_expr(value);
            super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy, &mut |expr| add_names(expr, &mut out)); out
        }
        fn input_names(value: &Expr) -> BTreeSet<String> {
            let mut out = BTreeSet::new(); value_nodes(value, &mut |expr| add_names(expr, &mut out)); out
        }

//! Quarry text from syntax, for source-aware diagnostics and pasteable rewrites.
use crate::ast::*;

type Source<'a> = dyn Fn(&Expr) -> Option<(String, bool)> + 'a;

/// Rewrite callers may embed an expression in another operator or clause.
/// Use the same precedence renderer as source excerpts, with a grouped root.
pub fn expression(expr: &Expr) -> String {
    let grouped = match expr {
        Expr::Binary { .. } | Expr::Unary { .. } | Expr::If { .. } => true,
        Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } =>
            read.filter.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.range.is_some(),
        _ => false,
    };
    with_source_at(expr, if grouped { 8 } else { 0 }, &|_| None)
}

pub(crate) fn with_source(expr: &Expr, source: &Source<'_>) -> String { with_source_at(expr, 0, source) }
pub(crate) fn with_source_at(expr: &Expr, minimum: u8, source: &Source<'_>) -> String { render(expr, minimum, source) }

pub fn quote(value: &str) -> String {
    format!("'{}'", value.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('\'', "\\'").replace('\n', "\\n").replace('\t', "\\t"))
}

fn priority(expr: &Expr) -> u8 {
    match expr {
        Expr::Binary { op, .. } => crate::parser::precedence(op).unwrap_or(0),
        Expr::If { .. } | Expr::Read(_) | Expr::Exists { .. } | Expr::Created { .. } | Expr::Merge(_) | Expr::Write { .. } | Expr::Walk { .. } => 0,
        Expr::Unary { .. } => 7,
        Expr::Assert { .. } | Expr::Shape { .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { .. } | Expr::Member { .. } => 8,
        _ => 9,
    }
}

fn render(expr: &Expr, minimum: u8, source: &Source<'_>) -> String {
    let wrap = |text: String, grouped: bool| if priority(expr) < minimum && !grouped { format!("({text})") } else { text };
    if let Some((text, grouped)) = source(expr) { return wrap(text, grouped); }
    let sub = |e| render(e, 0, source);
    let text = match expr {
        Expr::Name { name, .. } => name.clone(),
        Expr::Focal { .. } => ".".into(),
        Expr::Literal { value, .. } => match value {
            Literal::Str(s) => quote(s), Literal::Number(n) => n.clone(),
            Literal::Bool(b) => b.to_string(), Literal::Null => "null".into(),
        },
        Expr::Array { items, .. } => format!("[{}]", items.iter().map(sub).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),
        Expr::Object { fields, .. } => format!("{{ {} }}", object_fields(fields, source)),
        Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => {
            let value = render(value, 7, source);
            if op == "not" { format!("not {value}") }
            else if value.starts_with('-') { format!("-({value})") } else { format!("-{value}") }
        }
        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => {
            let p = priority(expr);
            let (l, r) = if op == "??" { (p + 1, p) } else { (p, p + 1) };
            format!("{} {op} {}", render(left, l, source), render(right, r, source))
        }
        Expr::Member { base, name, .. } => match base.as_ref() {
            Expr::Focal { .. } => format!(".{name}"),
            other => format!("{}.{name}", render(other, 8, source)),
        },
        Expr::Call { name, args, .. } => format!("{name}({})", args.iter().enumerate().map(|(n, arg)| {
            let value = sub(arg);
            if n > 0 && matches!(name.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket") && matches!(arg, Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(_), .. }) && source(arg).is_none() { format!("{value}ms") }
            else { value }
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),
        Expr::Read(read) => read_with(read, source),
        Expr::Merge(merge) => format!("merge {}{}{} {} {} by {}{}:", merge.table, merge.matching.as_ref().map_or(String::new(), |q| format!(" matching {}", sub(q))), merge.cursor.as_ref().map_or(String::new(), |c| format!(" after {}", sub(c))), if merge.order == Order::Asc { "first" } else { "last" }, merge.take, merge.index, merge.filter.as_ref().map_or(String::new(), |p| format!(" where {}", sub(p)))),
        Expr::Exists { read, .. } => format!("exists {}", read_with(read, source)),
        Expr::Created { read, .. } => format!("created {}", read_with(read, source)),
        Expr::Assert { value, code, message, .. } => format!("{} ! else {code}{}", render(value, 8, source), message.as_deref().map(|m| format!(" {}", quote(m))).unwrap_or_default()),
        Expr::ShapeUse { base, name, .. } => format!("{} {name}", render(base, 8, source)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => format!("{} {{ {} }}", render(base, 8, source), shape_with(fields, source)),
        Expr::Walk { table, seed, column, depth, .. } => format!("walk {table}[{}] up .{column} depth {depth}", sub(seed)),
        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => format!("{} if {} else {}", render(then, 1, source), render(condition, 1, source), sub(otherwise)),
        Expr::Write { op, table, keys, fields, if_absent, condition, .. } => {
            let mut out = format!("{} {table}", op.word());
            // `delete row` / `update row { … }` keep their row-variable spelling.
            if *op != WriteOp::Insert && !keys.is_empty() { out.push_str(&format!("[{}]", keys.iter().map(|k| key_part(k, source)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))); }
            if *op != WriteOp::Delete { out.push_str(&format!(" {{ {} }}", object_fields(fields, source))); }
            if *if_absent { out.push_str(" if absent"); }
            if let Some(condition) = condition { out.push_str(&format!(" if {}", sub(condition))); }
            out
        }
    };
    wrap(text, false)
}

pub fn read_expr(read: &ReadExpr) -> String { read_with(read, &|_| None) }

fn read_with(read: &ReadExpr, source: &Source<'_>) -> String {
    if let Some((text, _)) = source(&Expr::Read(read.clone())) { return text; }
    let sub = |e| render(e, 0, source);
    if !read.order_keys.is_empty() && read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some()) {
        let mut predicates = read.keys.iter().map(|key| format!(".{} = {}", key.column.as_ref().unwrap(), render(&key.value, 5, source))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        predicates.extend(read.seeks.iter().map(|bound| format!(".{} {} {}", bound.column, bound.op, render(&bound.value, 5, source))));
        if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { predicates.push(render(filter, if predicates.is_empty() { 0 } else { 3 }, source)); }
        let mut out = read.table.clone();
        if !predicates.is_empty() { out.push_str(&format!(" where {}", predicates.join(" and "))); }
        if let Some(matching) = &read.matching { out.push_str(&format!(" matching {}", sub(matching))); }
        if let Some(range) = &read.range { out.push_str(&format!(" {} {}", if range.order == Order::Asc { "first" } else { "last" }, range.take)); }
        out.push_str(&format!(" by {}", read.order_keys.iter().map(|key| format!(".{key}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")));
        if let Some(cursor) = read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_ref()) { out.push_str(&format!(" after {}", sub(cursor))); }
        return out;
    }
    let mut out = format!("{}[{}]", read.table, read.keys.iter().map(|key| key_part(key, source)).chain(read.seeks.iter().map(|b| format!("{} {} {}", b.column, b.op, sub(&b.value)))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
    if let Some(matching) = &read.matching { out.push_str(&format!(" matching {}", sub(matching))); }
    if let Some(range) = &read.range {
        if let Some(cursor) = &range.cursor { out.push_str(&format!(" after {}", sub(cursor))); }
        out.push_str(&format!(" {} {}", if range.order == Order::Asc { "first" } else { "last" }, range.take));
    }
    if let Some(index) = &read.index { out.push_str(&format!(" by {index}")); }
    if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { out.push_str(&format!(" where {}", sub(filter))); }
    out
}

fn key_part(part: &KeyPart, source: &Source<'_>) -> String {
    let value = render(&part.value, 0, source);
    match &part.column { Some(column) => format!("{column} = {value}"), None => value }
}

pub const KEYS: [&str; 8] = ["as", "op", "args", "expect", "data", "count", "save", "label"];

// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.3 — an unknown key is refused, never ignored: ignoring it was a false green.
pub(super) fn validate(case: &Json) -> Result<(), String> {
    let Json::Object(fields) = case else { return Err("each case is a JSON object: {\"op\":..,\"as\":..,\"args\":{..},\"expect\":..}".into()) };
    let valid = KEYS.iter().map(|key| format!("`{key}`")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
    for key in fields.keys() {
        if KEYS.contains(&key.as_str()) { continue; }
        let meant = match key.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "rows" | "length" | "len" | "size" | "n" | "total" | "counts" => Some("count"),
            "arguments" | "params" | "parameters" | "input" | "inputs" | "arg" => Some("args"),
            "persona" | "viewer" | "user" | "who" | "principal" => Some("as"),
            "operation" | "query" | "mutation" | "name" => Some("op"),
            "result" | "answer" | "value" | "rowsequal" | "equals" => Some("data"),
            "expected" | "expects" | "outcome" | "state" | "code" | "status" => Some("expect"),
            "description" | "desc" | "comment" | "note" | "title" => Some("label"),
            _ => None,
        };
        let hint = meant.map(|key| format!(" (did you mean `{key}`?)")).unwrap_or_default();
        return Err(format!("unknown key `{key}`{hint}; a case takes only {valid} (snapback4 guide testing)"));
    }
    for key in ["as", "op", "expect", "save", "label"] {
        if let Some(value) = fields.get(key) && !value.is_string() {
            let hint = if key == "expect" && value.is_object() { "; compare the answer with `data` (the whole answer) or `count` (its length) beside `expect`" } else { "" };
            return Err(format!("`{key}` is a string, got {value}{hint}"));
        }
    }
    if let Some(name) = fields.get("save").and_then(Json::as_str) && (name.is_empty() || name.contains('.') || name.starts_with('$')) {
        return Err(format!("save alias {name:?} must be nonempty with no dot or leading dollar; use a name such as r1, then reference $r1 or $r1.field"));
    }
    if let Some(value) = fields.get("count") && value.as_u64().is_none() { return Err(format!("`count` is a non-negative integer, got {value}")); }
    if let Some(value) = fields.get("args") && !value.is_object() && fields.get("expect").and_then(Json::as_str) != Some("E_INPUT") { return Err("\"args\" is an object".into()); }
    Ok(())
}

/// The query's N_NEVER_COMPLETE note, as `N_NEVER_COMPLETE at schema.q:L`.
fn never_complete(bound: &Bound, program: &snapback4_lang::BoundProgram) -> Option<String> {
    bound.notes.iter().chain(&program.notes)
        .find(|note| matches!(note.code.as_str(), "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE") && note.site == program.site)
        .map(|note| format!("{}: {} ({} at {}:{})", program.program.name, note.message, note.code, note.site.file, note.site.line))
}

/// Development evidence under a failing refusal: which layer refused, at which
/// line (LLP 3000.036 §3 B; the refusal itself never names the clause).
fn refusal_evidence(trace: &RefusalTrace, refusal: &Refusal, bound: &Bound, sources: &[(String, String)], explicit: Option<&str>, viewer: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let lookup = |file: &str| sources.iter().find(|(name, _)| name == file).map(|(_, text)| text.clone());
    let cause = trace.take();
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    match cause {
        Some(cause) if cause.terminal => {
            let record = super::run_refusal::record(&cause, &bound.refusal_sites);
            if let Some(text) = super::run_refusal::explain(&record, &lookup) { out.push(text.trim_start_matches("dev: ").to_owned()); }
            // Keep the operation source explanation alongside private table-rule evidence.
            if refusal.code == "NOT_FOUND" {
                if let Some(site) = refusal.site.as_deref() {
                    let resolved = operation_site(bound, site);
                    if let Some(line) = super::run_refusal::quote(resolved.as_deref().unwrap_or(site), &lookup) {
                        if !line.contains("else NOT_FOUND") {
                            let at = resolved.as_deref().unwrap_or(site).trim_end_matches(":1");
                            out.push(format!("NOT_FOUND is the write target's answer at `{line}` ({at}): the row is missing, or this viewer cannot read it and the table's write rule refused (a hidden row answers NOT_FOUND, never E_RULE; guide refusals)"));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if refusal.code == "E_RULE" && (cause.clause.starts_with("unique ") || cause.clause.starts_with("cap ")) {
                let held = if cause.clause.starts_with("unique ") { format!("a row with this `{}` key exists that this viewer cannot read", cause.clause) } else { format!("the `{}` group holds rows this viewer cannot read", cause.clause) };
                out.push(format!("hint: {held}; your own code would disclose it, so the engine refuses E_RULE: expect E_RULE here, or make those rows readable to the viewer (guide rules)"));
            }
        }
        // A program's own `! else CODE` or `require … else CODE`, at its
        // source line. When that site is a `t[x] !` lookup and t's read rule
        // hid a row in this operation, the row exists but reads as absent to
        // this viewer: a later `require` never ran.
        cause => if let Some(site) = refusal.site.as_deref().filter(|_| refusal.rule.as_deref() == Some(refusal.code.as_str())) {
            // An engine write-target refusal carries an operation-relative site
            // (`removeSale:2`); resolve it to the source line the program wrote.
            let resolved = operation_site(bound, site);
            let line = super::run_refusal::quote(resolved.as_deref().unwrap_or(site), &lookup);
            let shown = match (&line, &resolved) {
                (Some(line), Some(at)) => format!("`{line}` ({})", at.trim_end_matches(":1")),
                (Some(line), None) => format!("`{line}` ({site})"),
                (None, _) => site.to_owned(),
            };
            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.1 — wave 5: a NOT_FOUND the program never names is the write target's answer, not "the program's own refusal".
            if refusal.code == "NOT_FOUND" && !line.as_ref().is_some_and(|line| line.contains("else NOT_FOUND")) {
                out.push(format!("NOT_FOUND is the write target's answer at {shown}: the row is missing, or this viewer cannot read it and the table's write rule refused (a hidden row answers NOT_FOUND, never E_RULE; guide refusals)"));
                return out;
            }
            let hidden = cause.filter(|cause| !cause.terminal && cause.phase == "read rule"
                && bound.refusal_sites.contains_key(&(cause.table.clone(), format!("lookup {site}"))));
            match hidden {
                Some(cause) => {
                    let rule = bound.refusal_sites.get(&(cause.table.clone(), "read rule".into()));
                    let quoted = rule.map(|rule| match super::run_refusal::quote(rule, &lookup) { Some(text) => format!("`{text}` ({rule})"), None => rule.clone() }).unwrap_or_else(|| "its read rule".into());
                    let later = explicit.filter(|code| *code != refusal.code && sources.iter().any(|(_, text)| text.contains(&format!("else {code}")))).map(|code| format!("; a later `require … else {code}` never ran")).unwrap_or_default();
                    out.push(format!("{} is the program's own refusal at {shown}: the {} read rule {quoted} hid a row: hidden from {viewer} by {}'s read rule; a hidden row reads as absent to a lookup{later}", refusal.code, cause.table, cause.table));
                    out.push("hint: no code after the lookup can tell \"not yours\" from \"missing\": expect NOT_FOUND here, admit the viewer in the read rule, or write by id with no lookup so the table's update/delete rule decides (snapback4 guide testing, guide refusals)".into());
                }
                None => out.push(format!("{} is the program's own refusal at {shown}", refusal.code)),
            }
        },
    }
    out
}
        }
        // Unknown predicates are potentially satisfiable: this lint is not a theorem of reachability.
        RuleExpr::Ne(_, _) | RuleExpr::Compare(_, _, _) | RuleExpr::Not(_) => true,
    }
}
fn dependencies(rule: &RuleExpr, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Exists { table, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, .. } => { out.insert(table.clone()); }
        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => for item in items { dependencies(item, out); },
        RuleExpr::Eq(left, right) => out.extend(left.references().chain(right.references()).map(|(table, _)| table.to_owned())),
        _ => {}
    }
}
fn fixture_notes(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, fixture: Option<&String>) -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
    let path = fixture.map(std::path::PathBuf::from).unwrap_or_else(|| project.root.join("fixtures")).join(super::test_cases::FILE);
    let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path) else { return vec![]; };
    let mut notes = Vec::new();
    for (index, line) in text.lines().enumerate() {
        let line = line.trim();
        if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with("//") { continue; }
        let case: Result<serde_json::Value, _> = serde_json::from_str(line);
        // Shared cases may include operations added after the schema being checked.
        if case.as_ref().ok().and_then(|case| case["op"].as_str())
            .is_some_and(|op| !bound.programs.iter().any(|p| p.program.name == op)) { continue; }
        // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — any authored refusal case owns its deliberately invalid arguments.
        let expects_refusal = case.as_ref().ok().is_some_and(|case| {
            super::test_cases::validate(case).is_ok() && case["expect"].as_str().is_some_and(|expect| {
                !matches!(expect, "ok" | "complete" | "capped" | "sent")
                    && case["op"].as_str().is_some_and(|op| bound.programs.iter().any(|p| p.program.name == op))
            })
        });
        let check = || -> Result<(), String> {
            let case = case.as_ref().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let op = case["op"].as_str().ok_or("case needs op")?;
            let program = &bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == op).ok_or_else(|| format!("unknown operation {op}"))?.program;
            let empty = serde_json::json!({});
            let args = case.get("args").unwrap_or(&empty).as_object().ok_or("args must be an object")?;
            for (name, kind) in &program.args {
                if !args.contains_key(name) && !kind.is_optional() && !matches!(kind, ArgType::Cursor) { return Err(format!("{op}: missing argument {name}")); }
            }
            for (name, value) in args {
                let kind = program.args.get(name).ok_or_else(|| format!("{op} takes no argument {name}"))?;
                // Saved answers are runtime values; never claim a type failure for one.
                if saved(value) { continue; }
                let value = wire::from_json(kind, value).map_err(|e| format!("{name}: {e}"))?;
                validate::validate_arg(&bound.schema, kind, &value, name).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            Ok(())
        };
        if let Err(reason) = check() {
            // An authored refusal expectation may deliberately supply values
            // outside the operation's accepted input domain.
            if expects_refusal { continue; }
            notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&Site::new(&path.to_string_lossy(), index as u32 + 1, 1), "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT", reason,
                "update this case's arguments to the operation's current signature; run snapback4 test").guide("testing"));
        }
    }
    notes
}
fn saved(value: &serde_json::Value) -> bool {
    match value { serde_json::Value::String(s) => s.starts_with('$') && s.len() > 1, serde_json::Value::Array(items) => items.iter().any(saved), serde_json::Value::Object(items) => items.values().any(saved), _ => false }
}
